Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nMulti-method consensus framework for canary→downstream association robustness.\\n\\nFormalizes \\\"robust association\\\": a pair is robust only when ≥method_threshold\\nindependent statistical methods agree (each requiring ≥seed_threshold seeds\\nsignificant). Two-stage aggregation: seed→method verdict, then method→consensus.\\n\\nInput:  results/mvp/analysis/method_ablation.json\\nOutput: consensus classification, sensitivity heatmap, FPR/FNR table (JSON)\\n\\nDependencies: numpy, json (stdlib)\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport json\\nimport os\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\n\\nMETHOD_NAMES = [\\n    \\\"naive_pearson\\\",\\n    \\\"differenced_xcorr\\\",\\n    \\\"threshold_onset\\\",\\n    \\\"toda_yamamoto\\\",\\n    \\\"permutation_granger\\\",\\n]\\n\\n# differenced_xcorr is the FP ground truth (index 1)\\nDIFF_INDEX = 1\\n\\n\\ndef _to_native(obj):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Convert numpy types to Python native for JSON serialization.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if isinstance(obj, (np.integer,)):\\n        return int(obj)\\n    if isinstance(obj, (np.floating,)):\\n        return float(obj)\\n    if isinstance(obj, (np.bool_,)):\\n        return bool(obj)\\n    if isinstance(obj, np.ndarray):\\n        return obj.tolist()\\n    return obj\\n\\n\\nclass NumpyEncoder(json.JSONEncoder):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"JSON encoder that handles numpy types.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    def default(self, obj):\\n        native = _to_native(obj)\\n        if native is not obj:\\n            return native\\n        return super().default(obj)\\n\\n\\ndef load_sig_matrix(ablation_path, apply_fdr=True):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Load method_ablation.json into a boolean significance tensor.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        ablation_path: path to method_ablation.json\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        sig_matrix: np.ndarray, shape (5, 16, 4), dtype bool\\n                    axes = (method, pair, seed)\\n        pair_names: list of (canary, downstream) tuples, len 16\\n        method_names: list of str, len 5\\n        seeds: list of int, e.g. [42, 43, 44, 45]\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    with open(ablation_path) as f:\\n        data = json.load(f)\\n\\n    pairs = data[\\\"pairs\\\"]\\n    seeds = data[\\\"meta\\\"][\\\"seeds\\\"]\\n    n_m = len(METHOD_NAMES)\\n    n_p = len(pairs)\\n    n_s = len(seeds)\\n\\n    sig = np.zeros((n_m, n_p, n_s), dtype=bool)\\n    pair_names = []\\n\\n    for pi, pair in enumerate(pairs):\\n        pair_names.append((pair[\\\"canary\\\"], pair[\\\"downstream\\\"]))\\n        for si, seed_rec in enumerate(pair[\\\"per_seed\\\"]):\\n            for mi, method in enumerate(METHOD_NAMES):\\n    - Line 330: `"fpr_ground_truth": "differenced_xcorr (seed level)"` — the OLD `compute_fpr_fnr_diff` function still uses `differenced_xcorr` as FPR ground truth
   - Line 438: `"ground_truth": "alpha=0.0 (any significance is false positive by definition)"` — a NEW `compute_fpr_null_control` function was added (line 410) that uses α=0.0 as null control
   - A new function exists with the correct α=0.0 ground truth, but the main pathway on line 592 still calls the old `compute_fpr_fnr_diff` function, so the fix isn't actually being used.

2. **FDR fix in consensus_framework.py**: The `load_sig_matrix` function now correctly uses `significant_fdr`

by default — this looks correct (D029 fix applied).

3. **FDR fix in cross_alpha_analysis_v4.py**: There are no direct matches for `significant_fdr` in the script, which means D030 isn't directly applied. However, since it imports `load_sig_matrix` from `consensus_framework`, the consensus classification should already be using FDR. The Granger analysis within the script has its own `apply_fdr` function, so I need to verify whether that's being called correctly.

4. **GPU compliance**: Checking GPU utilization — currently around 14% on cuda:0 with about 14GB allocated.

Now I'm verifying the GPU assignments against the project whitelist and looking more closely at how `compute_fpr_null_control` is being used in the main output. I also need to trace through `cross_alpha_analysis_v4.py` more carefully to see how it handles FDR correction for individual alpha points.

adjusted[sorted_idx[i]] = min(sorted_p[i] * n / (i + 1),\\n                                       adjusted[sorted_idx[i + 1]])\\n    adjusted = np.minimum(adjusted, 1.0)\\n    return adjusted, ```text
Now I have the full picture. The `d028_fpr_null_control_full.json` output exists and provides:

1. **Null control FPR** at α=0.0:
   - naive: 100% FPR (raw and FDR)
   - differenced: 50% raw FPR, 37.5% FDR-corrected FPR
   - threshold: 62.5% FPR
   - permutation_granger: 0% FPR
   - TY: 0% FPR

2. **Phase 2 FDR-corrected significance rates** — this is the key data. 

Looking at the raw versus FDR-corrected results, I'm seeing a stark pattern where FDR correction is aggressively filtering out detections. At α=0.25, permutation_granger drops from 25% raw to 0% after FDR correction, losing 2 tests entirely, while TY falls from 62.5% to 37.5%. At α=0.50, permutation_granger again hits 0% after FDR correction despite 25% raw detection, but TY shows no change from its baseline 0%. This aggressive filtering is critical to understand when evaluating the phase 2 report's methodology section.

The phase 2 report is actually reporting raw significance rates, not FDR-corrected ones. The permutation_granger metrics show 25% at both α thresholds in the registry, but these are the raw rates—after FDR correction they'd both collapse to 0%. This creates a major inconsistency: D029 explicitly required FDR-corrected significance testing, yet the phase 2 report tables and registry metrics are recording the raw, uncorrected values. The D028 output file contains both versions, but the main report never actually uses the corrected figures. FDR correction is revealing a critical issue with the Phase 2 results. At α=0.25 and α=0.50, the permutation Granger method shows 25% significance in the original table, but after FDR correction both drop to 0% — meaning those headline numbers are wrong. The Ting-Yang method also gets hit hard, falling from 62.5% to 37.5% at α=0.25 and disappearing entirely at α=0.50. I need to update the Phase 2 table to reflect these corrected values.

The corrected results actually support the core claims better than I thought — at lower thresholds, strict methods fail completely, but at α=1.0 they suddenly show strong detection rates. This regime-dependent behavior is exactly what Claim 2 and Claim 3 are arguing for. However, I'm noticing that the main pipeline isn't actually using the FDR-corrected null control function, so I need to flag that the consensus framework is still calling the wrong function.

More critically, the Phase 2 report and registry are reporting raw significance rates instead of FDR-corrected ones, which directly violates what D029 required. The numbers look inflated — perm_granger shows 25% raw significance at α=0.25 and α=0.50, but the FDR-corrected version is 0% at both thresholds. This inconsistency needs to be fixed in both the report tables and the registry metrics. D029 and D030 don't explicitly cover `method_ablation_phase2.py`, but the broader intent across these fixes is that all statistics presented to the paper should be FDR-corrected. Since the Phase 2 report is the main output and will drive paper claims, and the FDR-corrected data already exists in Section 5, the fact that the primary tables still show raw rates is a discrepancy worth flagging as a warning before moving to paper writing.

The FDR fixes in `consensus_framework.py` and `cross_alpha_analysis_v4.py` are both applied correctly, and GPU assignments check out. I'm also noticing that the null control data from D028 shows concerning FPR rates—the differenced_xcorr at α=0.0 sits at 50% raw and 37.5% FDR, which validates D028's decision to switch ground truth sources.

Looking more closely at the registry metrics, I see that Phase 2 selectively records certain alpha values rather than all of them. At α=0.25, the raw rate is 62.5% but FDR is 37.5%, yet only `TY_sig_rate_a000` and `TY_sig_rate_a100` appear in the registry. Now I'm checking whether the Phase 2 report tables themselves are reporting raw or FDR values by comparing against the D028 output—at α=1.0, both the report and the output show perm_granger at 24/32, suggesting they align at this threshold.

But at lower alpha values like 0.25 and 0.50, the report shows perm_granger at 2/8 (25%) while the D028 output has raw=2 but FDR=0, confirming the report is displaying raw significance rates rather than FDR-corrected ones. This distinction matters substantially—applying FDR correction would flip the headline findings from 25% down to 0% at both α=0.25 and α=0.50.

So I'm marking this as a warning-level issue. The correct FDR data already exists in the system, and since we're still in Phase 2 before paper writing, there's time to align the numbers. The inconsistency actually strengthens the contrast between the naive and strict approaches, but I need to flag that directly copying Phase 2 numbers into the final paper without this correction would be problematic.

  "text": "## 审查报告 — worker_code_consensus_doc_fix (D028-D030 修复 + method_ablation_phase2)\n\n**结论**: WARNING\n\n### 发现\n\n**1. [severity: warning] Phase 2 报告主表使用 raw significance，与 D029 FDR 要求不一致**\n`method_ablation_phase2_report.md` Section 1 的 method×alpha 显著性率表使用 raw p-value，未用 FDR-corrected。关键差异（来自 `d028_fpr_null_control_full.json` 交叉验证）：\n- α=0.25 perm_granger: 报告 2/8 (25%) → FDR 后 **0/8 (0%)**\n- α=0.50 perm_granger: 报告 2/8 (25%) → FDR 后 **0/8 (0%)**\n- α=0.25 TY: 报告 5/8 (62%) → FDR 后 **3/8 (37.5%)**\n\nRegistry 指标 `perm_granger_sig_rate_a025: 0.25` 和 `perm_granger_sig_rate_a050: 0.25` 也是 raw 值。FDR 数据已存在（Section 5 flip counts + d028 output），但主表未更新。论文写作时若直接引用这些数字会与 D029 FDR 承诺矛盾。注：FDR 后结论方向不变且实际强化了 Claim 2/3（strict methods 在 α≤0.50 全部归零 vs naive 100%）。\n\n**3. [severity: info] D029 FDR 修复正确落地**\n`consensus_framework.py` 的 `load_sig_matrix(apply_fdr=True)` 正确读取 `significant_fdr` 字段，`threshold_onset` 例外处理合理（非统计检验无 FDR 版本）。\n\n**4. [severity: info] D030 cross_alpha_analysis_v4.py FDR 一致性已实现**\n该脚本通过 `load_sig_matrix(apply_fdr=True)`（line 362）和自有 `apply_fdr()` 函数（line 806）实现全局 BH FDR 校正。\n\n**5. [severity: info] method_ablation_phase2 registry 数据一致性验证通过**\nRegistry 中所有指标（naive_sig_rate_overall=1.0, perm_granger 各 α 点, TY 各点, kappa=0.185, flip rates）与 `method_ablation_phase2_report.md` 完全吻合。Spec 字段完整（hypothesis, command, server, data_path, data_count, seed, log_path）。\n\n**6. [severity: info] GPU 合规**\nnvidia-smi: cuda:0 14% util / 14.4GB, cuda:1 19% util / 14.4GB。plan_006_s42 → cuda:0, alpha_sweep_a075_s42 → cuda:1，均在项目白名单内。Pythia-410M (410M params) 在 96GB RTX PRO 6000 上低显存占用属预期。\n\n### 建议\n1. 将 `consensus_framework.py main()` 切换为调用 `compute_fpr_null_control`（或同时输出两版 FPR 供对比），更新注释和 `DIFF_INDEX` 残留\n2. Phase 2 报告主表增加 FDR-corrected 列，或将主表改为 FDR-corrected、raw 放附注。Registry 指标名应标明 raw/fdr\n3. 以上两项在论文写作前必须完成，否则 paper claims 与统计方法声明不对齐"

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_4/projects/distrib_canary_collapse/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — distrib_canary_collapse\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n- Primary: AAAI 2027 (expected deadline ~August 2026)\\n- Fallback: EMNLP 2026 Findings (ARR October cycle if AAAI timeline tight)\\n- Note: EMNLP 2026 Main ARR May deadline已过；AAAI 2027 给出充足实验时间\\n\\n## Narrative Structure (D027: Option B reframe)\\n\\n**主线**：方法论陷阱 + 多方法共识框架。Canary prediction 为系统性 case study。\\n**Claim 呈现顺序**：论文中 Claim 3 领衔 → Claim 1 (case study) → Claim 2 (regime map)。\\n**内部编号保持不变**（避免引用混乱），论文呈现时重排。\\n\\n## Core Claims (3 条，内部编号不变)\\n\\n1. **Canary→Diversity Granger-Causal Precedence (论文中作为 Case Study Finding)**: token_entropy 对 diversity 退化（distinct_1/2）具有 Granger-causal precedence——领先 1-2 代，差分后仍显著，证据来自多方法一致性：cross-correlation（lag=-1, std=0.0, |r|>0.99, p<0.004, 4/4 seed）、差分交叉相关（显著）、threshold onset（canary gen 1-2, diversity gen 3-4）、**permutation Granger（差分后仍 4/4 seed 显著，F=612-2615）**。论文可用措辞 (D025/D026 更新)：*\\\"We demonstrate Granger-causal precedence of canary metrics at α=1.0 (24/24 FDR-sig, 4 seeds). Consistent temporal precedence (cross-correlation lag=+1, r=0.80–0.94) is observed across all contamination levels, though formal Granger tests at intermediate α do not reach FDR-corrected significance at T=11 (power analysis: §X).\\\"*升级上限：Granger ≠ true causality（Granger 1969），Discussion 一句话声明；双变量模型无法排除未观测混淆因子（详见 Reviewer Concerns 表）。ECE 在 cross-correlation 和 permutation Granger 下同样领先（差分后 4/4 SIG），但跨方法一致性弱于 token_entropy，地位为 conditional secondary canary 待 plan_006 确认（详见约束 #9）。注意：TY Granger 在 N=11 下不可用于区分真/伪关联（见 Claim 3），核心证据链基于多方法一致性而非单一 TY 背书。\\n2. **Regime-Dependent Predictability**: 可预测性取决于 collapse 维度和 contamination regime——canary→diversity 在差分后仍 robust（6/6 pair × 4/4 seed），但 canary→perplexity **经差分检验确认为伪回归**（0/4 seed, F≈0, p=0.99），未差分时的\\\"信号\\\"（F=16-36）完全由共享非平稳趋势驱动（D017）。ECE→perplexity 的反向因果同理存疑。**Claim 2 reframing (D022)**: 从\\\"不同 α 下 lead time 不同\\\"升级为\\\"不同 α 下 **detectability** 不同\\\"——α=0.25 差分 Granger 0/8 FDR sig vs α=1.0 的 24/24，同一 pair 的统计可检测性随 contamination level 剧变。**Evidence strategy (Reviewer 共识)**：Primary = raw drift magnitude dose-response（distinct_1: -14%@α=0 → -40%@α=0.25 → ~-93%@α=1.0），方法无关、不可争议；Secondary = 差分 Granger FDR sig rate vs α，展示 detectability boundary。**不用 naive_xcorr 做 primary**——与 Claim 3 自相矛盾。Predictability regime map 划定可预测/不可预测边界（\\\"phase diagram\\\" 需 ≥6 个 α 值 + uncertainty interval 才成立，当前 5 个 α 值下用 \\\"regime map\\\"）。\\n3. **Method Selection Sensitivity and Regime-Dependent Validity**（升级自 Methodological Rigor；D022 升级候选：method × regime interaction）: 在典型 generation 数（N≈10）下，lead-lag 结论对分析方法和超参数高度敏感，且**同一方法在不同 α regime 下结论翻转**（diff Granger: α=1.0 24/24 FDR sig → α=0.25 0/8）。升级措辞候选：\\\"Method sensitivity is compounded by regime dependence: the same statistical test applied to the same metric pair can yield significant or null results depending on contamination level\\\"。**三层证据结构**：**(Layer 1)** naive Pearson 找到 16/16 显著——方法宽松时一切 \\\"显著\\\"；**(Layer 2)** 一阶差分翻转整条 perplexity 通道（8/8→0/8 SIG）而 diversity 通道不变（12/12→12/12）——一个方法论选择决定通道存亡（D017）；**(Layer 3)** TY d_max sensitivity 使结论在 ~0%↔94% spurious rate 间震荡—\\n\\n... [截断: 省略 12139 字符，传 max_chars=0 获取完整内容]\", \"total_chars\": 15139, \"truncated\": true}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_4/projects/distrib_canary_collapse/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — distrib_canary_collapse\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n- Primary: AAAI 2027 (expected deadline ~August 2026)\\n- Fallback: EMNLP 2026 Findings (ARR October cycle if AAAI timeline tight)\\n- Note: EMNLP 2026 Main ARR May deadline已过；AAAI 2027 给出充足实验时间\\n\\n## Narrative Structure (D027: Option B reframe)\\n\\n**主线**：方法论陷阱 + 多方法共识框架。Canary prediction 为系统性 case study。\\n**Claim 呈现顺序**：论文中 Claim 3 领衔 → Claim 1 (case study) → Claim 2 (regime map)。\\n**内部编号保持不变**（避免引用混乱），论文呈现时重排。\\n\\n## Core Claims (3 条，内部编号不变)\\n\\n1. **Canary→Diversity Granger-Causal Precedence (论文中作为 Case Study Finding)**: token_entropy 对 diversity 退化（distinct_1/2）具有 Granger-causal precedence——领先 1-2 代，差分后仍显著，证据来自多方法一致性：cross-correlation（lag=-1, std=0.0, |r|>0.99, p<0.004, 4/4 seed）、差分交叉相关（显著）、threshold onset（canary gen 1-2, diversity gen 3-4）、**permutation Granger（差分后仍 4/4 seed 显著，F=612-2615）**。论文可用措辞 (D025/D026 更新)：*\\\"We demonstrate Granger-causal precedence of canary metrics at α=1.0 (24/24 FDR-sig, 4 seeds). Consistent temporal precedence (cross-correlation lag=+1, r=0.80–0.94) is observed across all contamination levels, though formal Granger tests at intermediate α do not reach FDR-corrected significance at T=11 (power analysis: §X).\\\"*升级上限：Granger ≠ true causality（Granger 1969），Discussion 一句话声明；双变量模型无法排除未观测混淆因子（详见 Reviewer Concerns 表）。ECE 在 cross-correlation 和 permutation Granger 下同样领先（差分后 4/4 SIG），但跨方法一致性弱于 token_entropy，地位为 conditional secondary canary 待 plan_006 确认（详见约束 #9）。注意：TY Granger 在 N=11 下不可用于区分真/伪关联（见 Claim 3），核心证据链基于多方法一致性而非单一 TY 背书。\\n2. **Regime-Dependent Predictability**: 可预测性取决于 collapse 维度和 contamination regime——canary→diversity 在差分后仍 robust（6/6 pair × 4/4 seed），但 canary→perplexity **经差分检验确认为伪回归**（0/4 seed, F≈0, p=0.99），未差分时的\\\"信号\\\"（F=16-36）完全由共享非平稳趋势驱动（D017）。ECE→perplexity 的反向因果同理存疑。**Claim 2 reframing (D022)**: 从\\\"不同 α 下 lead time 不同\\\"升级为\\\"不同 α 下 **detectability** 不同\\\"——α=0.25 差分 Granger 0/8 FDR sig vs α=1.0 的 24/24，同一 pair 的统计可检测性随 contamination level 剧变。**Evidence strategy (Reviewer 共识)**：Primary = raw drift magnitude dose-response（distinct_1: -14%@α=0 → -40%@α=0.25 → ~-93%@α=1.0），方法无关、不可争议；Secondary = 差分 Granger FDR sig rate vs α，展示 detectability boundary。**不用 naive_xcorr 做 primary**——与 Claim 3 自相矛盾。Predictability regime map 划定可预测/不可预测边界（\\\"phase diagram\\\" 需 ≥6 个 α 值 + uncertainty interval 才成立，当前 5 个 α 值下用 \\\"regime map\\\"）。\\n3. **Method Selection Sensitivity and Regime-Dependent Validity**（升级自 Methodological Rigor；D022 升级候选：method × regime interaction）: 在典型 generation 数（N≈10）下，lead-lag 结论对分析方法和超参数高度敏感，且**同一方法在不同 α regime 下结论翻转**（diff Granger: α=1.0 24/24 FDR sig → α=0.25 0/8）。升级措辞候选：\\\"Method sensitivity is compounded by regime dependence: the same statistical test applied to the same metric pair can yield significant or null results depending on contamination level\\\"。**三层证据结构**：**(Layer 1)** naive Pearson 找到 16/16 显著——方法宽松时一切
